K_{1}
produces a higher order term when
\partial_{3}^{4}\partial_{t}
falls on
J^{-1}
. We invoke
\partial_{t}J=J\text{div}_{A}v
to get

\displaystyle-\left[J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial),\partial_{3}^{4}\partial_{t}\right]\eta

(4.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{3}^{4}\partial_{t}(J^{-1})\,(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta+\sum_{N=0}^{3}\partial_{3}^{N}\partial_{t}(J^{-1})\,\partial_{3}^{4-N}b_{0}\cdot\partial\eta+\sum_{M=0}^{3}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{3}^{M}(J^{-1}b_{0}^{l})\,\partial_{l}\partial_{3}^{4-M}\eta\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-J^{-1}\partial_{3}^{4}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left([\partial_{3}^{4},J^{-1}]\text{div}_{A}v\right)(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta+\sum_{N=0}^{3}\partial_{3}^{N}\partial_{t}(J^{-1})(\partial_{3}^{4-N}b_{0}^{l})(\partial_{l}\eta)+\sum_{M=0}^{3}\partial_{t}\left(\partial_{3}^{M}(J^{-1}b_{0}^{l})\partial_{l}\partial_{3}^{4-M}\eta\right)}_{KL_{1}},

and thus

\displaystyle K_{1}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y}_{K_{11}}+\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot(KL_{1})

(4.12)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle K_{11}+\|J\|_{L^{\infty}}\|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4}\|KL_{1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle K_{11}+P\left(\|(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|\eta\|_{4},\|b_{0}\|_{4}\right).

Summarizing (
4.9
)-(
4.12
), we get the following estimates

\int_{\Omega}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\partial_{3}^{4}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\mathbf{V}\,\mathrm{d}y\lesssim-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Omega}J\left|\partial_{3}^{4}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y+K_{11}+P\left(\|(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|\eta\|_{4},\|b_{0}\|_{4},\|v\|_{4}\right).

(4.13)

We note that the term
K_{11}
cannot be directly controlled, but will be cancelled by another term produced by
-\int_{\Omega}(\nabla_{\hat{A}}\mathbf{Q})\cdot\mathbf{V}
.

Next we analyze the second integral on the RHS of (
4.7
). Integraing by parts and invoking Piola’s identity
\partial_{l}\hat{A}^{li}=0
, we get

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}(\nabla_{\hat{A}}\mathbf{Q})\cdot\mathbf{V}\,\mathrm{d}y=\int_{\Omega}J\mathbf{Q}(\nabla_{A}\cdot\mathbf{V})\,\mathrm{d}y-\int_{\Gamma}J\mathbf{Q}A^{li}N_{l}\mathbf{V}_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}=:I+IB.

(4.14)

Plugging (
4.2
) and (
4.4
) as well as
Q=q+\frac{1}{2}|b|^{2}
into
I
, we get

\displaystyle I=

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\partial_{3}^{4}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y+\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right|^{2}\right)\partial_{3}^{4}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y

(4.15)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\partial_{l}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}^{4}Q\,C(v)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4}.

The term
I_{4}
can be directly controlled by using (
4.5
)

I_{4}\lesssim\|Q\|_{4}\|C(v)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{4})\|Q\|_{4}\|v\|_{4}.

(4.16)

The term
I_{1}
gives the energy of
q
by invoking
\text{div}_{A}v=-\dfrac{\partial_{t}R}{R}=-\dfrac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{t}q

\displaystyle I_{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\partial_{3}^{4}\left(\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{t}q\right)\,\mathrm{d}y=-\int_{\Omega}\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\partial_{3}^{4}\partial_{t}q\,\mathrm{d}y-\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}q\left(\left[\partial_{3}^{4},\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right]\partial_{t}q\right)\,\mathrm{d}y

(4.17)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Omega}\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\left|\partial_{3}^{4}q\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y+\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\left(\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right)\left|\partial_{3}^{4}q\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y-\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}q\left(\left[\partial_{3}^{4},\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right]\partial_{t}q\right)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Omega}\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\left|\partial_{3}^{4}q\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y+P(\|q\|_{8,*},\|\rho_{0}\|_{4},\|\eta\|_{4}).

The term
I_{2}
will produce another higher order term to cancel with
K_{11}

\displaystyle I_{2}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\Omega}J\partial_{3}^{4}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}(\text{div}_{A}v)}_{\text{exactly cancel with }K_{11}}\,\mathrm{d}y

(4.18)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}\sum_{N=1}^{3}\binom{4}{N}J\partial_{3}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\partial_{3}^{4-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-K_{11}-\int_{\Omega}\sum_{N=1}^{3}\binom{4}{N}J\partial_{3}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\partial_{3}^{4-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}\left(\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{t}q\right)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-K_{11}-\int_{\Omega}\sum_{N=1}^{3}\binom{4}{N}\left(\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right)\partial_{3}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\partial_{3}^{4-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}\partial_{t}q\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}\sum_{N=1}^{3}\binom{4}{N}J\partial_{3}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\partial_{3}^{4-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\left(\left[\partial_{3}^{4},\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right]\partial_{t}q\right)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle-K_{11}+I_{21}+I_{22}.

We should control
I_{21}
by integrating
\partial_{t}
by parts under time integral

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}I_{21}\overset{\partial_{t}}{=}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{N=1}^{3}\binom{4}{N}\partial_{t}\left(\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right)\partial_{3}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\partial_{3}^{4-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\mathrm{d}y

(4.19)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{N=1}^{3}\binom{4}{N}\left(\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right)\partial_{t}\partial_{3}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\partial_{3}^{4-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\sum_{N=1}^{3}\binom{4}{N}\left(\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right)\partial_{3}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\partial_{3}^{4-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\mathrm{d}y\bigg|^{T}_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}P(\|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{4},\|\partial_{t}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\|_{3},\|q\|_{4})+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{3}^{2}\|\partial_{3}^{4}q\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t+\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}^{4}q\|_{0}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\|_{3}^{2}\leq\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t+\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}^{4}q\|_{0}^{2}.

Then
I_{22}
can be directly controlled since at most three
\partial_{3}
’s fall on
\partial_{t}q
.

I_{22}\lesssim\|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{3}^{2}\|q\|_{7,*}.

(4.20)

The term
I_{3}
should also be controlled under time integral. We have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}I_{3}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\partial_{l}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}\partial_{t}q\,\mathrm{d}y+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\partial_{l}Q\,\left[\partial_{3}^{4},\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right]\partial_{t}q}_{L_{2}}\,\mathrm{d}y

(4.21)

\displaystyle\overset{\partial_{t}}{=}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\left(\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\,\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\partial_{l}Q\right)\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\mathrm{d}y+\int_{\Omega}\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\partial_{l}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}q\,\mathrm{d}y\bigg|^{T}_{0}+L_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}+\left\|\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\,A\,\partial Q\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{3}^{4}q\|_{0}\|\partial_{3}^{4}\eta\|_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P\left(\|q\|_{8,*},\|\eta\|_{4},\|v\|_{4},\|\rho_{0}\|_{4}\right)\,\mathrm{d}t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t+\left\|\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\,A\,\partial Q\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{3}^{4}q\|_{0}\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{3}^{4}v(t)\|_{0}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(t))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t,

where we use
\partial^{4}\eta|_{t=0}=0
in the last step. Summarizing (
4.16
)-(
4.21
) and choosing
\varepsilon>0
suitably small, we get the estimates of
I
under time integral

\int_{0}^{T}I\,\mathrm{d}t\lesssim-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\left|\partial_{3}^{4}q\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y\bigg|^{T}_{0}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(t))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(4.22)

4.3
Boundary estimates

To finish the estimates of purely non-weighted normal derivative, it remains to control the boundary integral
IB
in (
4.14
) which reads

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}J\mathbf{Q}A^{li}N_{l}\mathbf{V}_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\,\partial_{3}^{4}Q\,\mathbf{V}_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

(4.23)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\partial_{3}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}v_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}-\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\partial_{3}Q\,(\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{r}~A^{mr}~\partial_{m}v_{i})\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IB_{0}+IB_{1}+IB_{2}.

First,
IB_{1}
will produce the boundary energy with the help of Rayleigh-Taylor sign condition (
1.8
) and the error terms will be cancelled with
IB_{2}
. In specific, we have

\displaystyle IB_{1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\left(-\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)JA^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\partial_{3}^{4}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

(4.24)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Gamma}\left(-J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)\left|A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\left(J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)\left|A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}+\int_{\Gamma}\left(-J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)\partial_{t}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IB_{11}+IB_{12}+IB_{13}.

Invoking Rayleigh-Taylor sign condition, we get

\int_{0}^{T}IB_{11}\,\mathrm{d}t\lesssim-\frac{c_{0}}{4}\int_{\Gamma}\left|A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\bigg|^{T}_{0},

(4.25)

and thus the term
IB_{12}
can be directly controlled by the boundary energy

IB_{12}\lesssim|\partial_{t}(J\partial_{3}Q)|_{L^{\infty}}\left|A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\lesssim P(\mathcal{E}(t)).

(4.26)

Then we plug
\partial_{t}A^{3i}=-A^{3r}~\partial_{m}v_{r}~A^{mi}
into
IB_{13}
to get

\displaystyle IB_{13}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\left(\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)A^{3r}\partial_{m}v_{r}\,\hat{A}^{mi}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime},

(4.27)

and this term exactly cancel with
IB_{2}
if we replace the indices
(r,i)
by
(i,r)
.

It now remains to control
IB_{0}
. We have

IB_{0}=-\int_{\Gamma}N_{3}J~\partial_{3}^{4}Q~(A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}v_{i})\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}+\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\partial_{3}^{4}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{lp}\partial_{l}v_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}=:IB_{01}+IB_{02}.

(4.28)

To control
IB_{0}
, we shall differentiate the following relations

\displaystyle A^{3i}\partial_{3}v_{i}=

\displaystyle\text{div}_{A}v-A^{1i}\overline{\partial}{}_{1}v_{i}-A^{2i}\overline{\partial}{}_{2}v_{i}=-\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{t}q-A^{1i}\overline{\partial}{}_{1}v_{i}-A^{2i}\overline{\partial}{}_{2}v_{i}.

(4.29)

In
IB_{01}
, we use the relation (
4.29
) to get

\displaystyle A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}v_{i}=

\displaystyle\partial_{3}^{3}(A^{3i}\partial_{3}v_{i})-\partial_{3}^{3}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}v_{i}-3\partial_{3}^{2}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{2}v_{i}-3\partial_{3}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{3}v_{i}

(4.30)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\partial_{3}^{3}\left(\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{t}q\right)-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\partial_{3}^{3}(A^{Li}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v_{i})-\partial_{3}^{3}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}v_{i}-3\partial_{3}^{2}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{2}v_{i}-3\partial_{3}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{3}v_{i},

and thus
IB_{01}
becomes

\displaystyle IB_{01}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}N_{3}J~\partial_{3}^{4}Q~\partial_{3}^{3}\left(\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{t}q\right)+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}N_{3}J~\partial_{3}^{4}Q~\partial_{3}^{3}(A^{Li}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v_{i})

(4.31)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Gamma}N_{3}J\partial_{3}^{4}Q~\left(\partial_{3}^{3}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}v_{i}+3\partial_{3}^{2}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{2}v_{i}+3\partial_{3}A^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{3}v_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IB_{011}+IB_{012}+IB_{013}.

In
IB_{012}
, since
A^{Li}
has the form
\partial_{3}\eta\times\overline{\partial}{}\eta
, the highest order term contains
\partial_{3}^{3}A^{Li}=\partial_{3}^{4}\eta\times\overline{\partial}{}\eta+\cdots
which cannot be directly controlled. However, this term can produce cancellation with
IB_{02}
. We have

\displaystyle\partial_{3}^{3}A^{Li}=

\displaystyle-\partial_{3}^{2}(A^{Lp}\,\partial_{3}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})

(4.32)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-A^{Lp}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}A^{3i}-\sum_{M=1}^{2}A^{Lp}\partial_{3}^{3}\overline{\partial}{}_{M}\eta_{p}A^{Mi}-[\partial_{3}^{2},A^{Lp}A^{mi}]\partial_{3}\partial_{m}\eta_{p},

and thus
IB_{012}
can be written as

\displaystyle IB_{012}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\partial_{3}^{4}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{Lp}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v_{i}

(4.33)

\displaystyle-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}N_{3}J\partial_{3}^{4}Q\left(\sum_{M=1}^{2}A^{Lp}\partial_{3}^{3}\overline{\partial}{}_{M}\eta_{p}\,A^{Mi}+[\partial_{3}^{2},A^{Lp}A^{mi}]\partial_{3}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\right).

(4.34)

On the other hand, we write
IB_{02}
as

\displaystyle IB_{02}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\partial_{3}^{4}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\partial_{3}v_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

(4.35)

\displaystyle+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\partial_{3}^{4}Q\,\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}\,A^{Lp}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}v_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}.

(4.36)

Therefore, (
4.36
) exactly cancels with the main term (
4.33
) in
IB_{012}
.

Now it remains to control
IB_{011},IB_{013}
and (
4.34
), (
4.35
). Invoking the relation

\displaystyle\hat{A}^{3i}\partial_{3}Q=

\displaystyle-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\hat{A}^{Li}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q-\rho_{0}\partial_{t}v^{i}+(b_{0}\cdot\partial)(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta^{i}),

(4.37)

we get

\displaystyle\hat{A}^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}Q=

\displaystyle\partial_{3}^{3}(\hat{A}^{3i}\partial_{3}Q)-\partial_{3}^{3}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}Q-3\partial_{3}^{2}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{2}Q-3\partial_{3}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{3}Q

(4.38)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{3}^{3}\left(-\rho_{0}\partial_{t}v^{i}+(b_{0}\cdot\partial)(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\partial_{3}^{3}(\hat{A}^{Li}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q)

\displaystyle-\partial_{3}^{3}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}Q-3\partial_{3}^{2}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{2}Q-3\partial_{3}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{3}Q.

Note that

•

The term
\hat{A}^{3i}
is of the form
\overline{\partial}{}\eta\times\overline{\partial}{}\eta
, so the leading order term in
\partial_{3}^{3}A^{3i}
should be
(\partial_{3}^{3}\overline{\partial}{}\eta)(\overline{\partial}{}\eta)
.

•

The highest order term in
\partial_{3}^{3}(\hat{A}^{Li}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q)
is
\partial_{3}^{3}\hat{A}^{Li}\,\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q=0
due to
\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q|_{\Gamma=0}
.

•

The highest order term in
\partial_{3}^{3}((b_{0}\cdot\partial)(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta))
is
(b_{0}\cdot\overline{\partial}{})\partial_{3}^{3}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)
because
b_{0}^{3}|_{\Gamma}=0
makes
(b_{0}\cdot\partial)
tangential on the boundary.

Therefore, we can rewrite
\partial_{3}^{4}Q
to be the terms of at most 3 normal derivatives and one tangential derivative:

\displaystyle\partial_{3}^{4}Q=

\displaystyle\underbrace{J^{-1}\hat{A}^{3i}\partial_{3}\eta_{i}}_{=1}\partial_{3}^{4}Q=J^{-1}\partial_{3}\eta_{i}(\hat{A}^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}Q)

(4.39)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{-1}\partial_{3}\eta_{i}\bigg(\partial_{3}^{3}\left(-\rho_{0}\partial_{t}v^{i}+(b_{0}\cdot\overline{\partial}{})(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right)-\sum_{L=1}^{2}\sum_{N=0}^{2}\binom{3}{N}(\partial_{3}^{N}\hat{A}^{Li})(\partial_{3}^{3-N}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q)

\displaystyle-\partial_{3}^{3}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}Q-3\partial_{3}^{2}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{2}Q-3\partial_{3}\hat{A}^{3i}\,\partial_{3}^{3}Q\bigg).

In (
4.35
), we need to rewrite
A^{3p}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}
by using
A^{3p}\partial_{3}\eta_{p}=1
in
\bar{\Omega}
(and thus
\partial_{3}^{3}(A^{3p}\partial_{3}\eta_{p})=0
)

\displaystyle A^{3p}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{p}=-\partial_{3}^{3}A^{3p}\,\partial_{3}\eta_{p}-3\partial_{3}^{2}A^{3p}\,\partial_{3}^{2}\eta_{p}-3\partial_{3}A^{3p}\,\partial_{3}^{3}\eta_{p}.

(4.40)

In the light of (
4.38
)-(
4.40
), we are able to write
IB_{011},IB_{013}
and (
4.34
), (
4.35
) in the form of

\int_{\Gamma}N_{3}(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)h\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}+\text{lower order terms},

(4.41)

where
\mathfrak{D}=\overline{\partial}{}
or
\partial_{t}
or
b_{0}\cdot\overline{\partial}{}
, and
f,g
can be
\eta,v,q,J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta
, and
h
contains at most first order derivative of
\eta,v
. Then (
4.41
) can be controlled in the following way

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}N_{3}(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)h\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{4}\mathfrak{D}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)h-\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}f)(\partial_{3}^{4}\mathfrak{D}g)h-\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)(\partial_{3}h)\right)

(4.42)

\displaystyle\overset{\mathfrak{D}}{=}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{4}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}^{2}g)h-\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{4}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)(\mathfrak{D}h)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}^{2}f)(\partial_{3}^{4}g)h+\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}f)(\partial_{3}^{4}g)(\mathfrak{D}h)-\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)(\partial_{3}h)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\partial_{3}^{4}f\|_{0}+\|\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}^{2}f\|_{0})(\|\partial_{3}^{4}g\|_{0}+\|\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}^{2}g\|_{0})\|\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|f\|_{8,*}\|g\|_{8,*}\|h\|_{3},

which gives the control of
IB_{011},IB_{013}
and (
4.34
), (
4.35
).

Remark
.

If we integrate
\mathfrak{D}=\partial_{t}
by parts in (
4.42
) (such term appears in a leading order term
\partial_{3}^{3}\partial_{t}v
in
\partial_{3}^{4}Q
), then we should proceed the estimate under time integral and also consider the terms like
\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{4}f)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)h
which can be controlled by

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{3}^{4}v)(\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g)h\lesssim\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{8\varepsilon}\|\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}g\|_{0}^{4}+\frac{1}{8\varepsilon}\|h\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}

(4.43)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{8\varepsilon}\left(\|g(0)\|_{7,*}^{4}+\|h(0)\|_{2}^{4}+\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{3}^{3}\mathfrak{D}\partial_{t}g(t)\|_{0}^{4}+\|\partial_{t}h(t)\|_{2}^{4}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\|g\|_{8,*},\|h\|_{5,*})\,\mathrm{d}t.

According to (
4.42
)-(
4.43
), we can finalize the estimates of the boundary integral
IB
as follows

IB\lesssim\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}-\frac{c_{0}}{4}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Gamma}\left|A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}+P(\mathcal{E}(t)).

(4.44)

4.4
Energy estimates of purely normal derivatives

Now, (
4.44
) together with (
4.7
), (
4.8
), (
4.13
), (
4.22
) gives the estimates of Alinhac good unknowns of
v,Q
in the case of purely non-weighted normal derivatives

\|\mathbf{V}\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\partial_{3}^{4}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}^{2}+\|\partial_{3}^{4}q\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|_{t=T}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(4.45)

Finally, by the definition of Alinhac good unknown (
4.2
) and
\partial_{3}^{4}\eta|_{t=0}=\mathbf{0}
,
\partial_{3}^{4}v
is controlled by

\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}\lesssim\|\mathbf{V}\|_{0}^{2}+\|a\partial v\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t\lesssim\|\mathbf{V}\|_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t,

(4.46)

and thus by choosing
\varepsilon>0
sufficiently small, we get

\|\partial_{3}^{4}v\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\partial_{3}^{4}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}^{2}+\|\partial_{3}^{4}q\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\partial_{3}^{4}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|_{t=T}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(4.47)

5
Control of purely tangential derivatives

Now we consider the purely tangential derivatives. In this case, the top order derivative becomes
\partial_{*}^{I}=\partial_{t}^{i_{0}}\overline{\partial}{}_{1}^{i_{1}}\overline{\partial}{}_{2}^{i_{2}}
with
i_{0}+i_{1}+i_{2}=8
. We will prove the following estimates by a modified Alinhac good unknown method.

Proposition 5.1
.

The following energy inequality holds for any sufficiently small
\varepsilon>0

\sum_{i_{3}=i_{4}=0}\|\partial_{*}^{I}v\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\partial_{*}^{I}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}^{2}+\|\partial_{*}^{I}q\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\partial_{*}^{I}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|_{t=T}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\partial_{3}\partial_{t}^{6}v\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(5.1)

For simplicity, we mainly study the case
i_{0}=0
, i.e.,
\partial_{*}^{I}=\overline{\partial}{}_{1}^{i_{1}}\overline{\partial}{}_{2}^{i_{2}}
with
i_{1}+i_{2}=8
. For sake of clean notations, we denote
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}=\overline{\partial}{}_{1}^{i_{1}}\overline{\partial}{}_{2}^{i_{2}}
. In fact, most of the steps of the proof in this section are completely applicable to the case of
i_{0}>0
.

5.1
The case of full spatial derivatives

5.1.1
Derivation of “modified Alinhac good unknowns” in anisotropic Sobolev space

We still use Alinhac good unknowns to control the tangential derivatives. However, we cannot directly replace
\partial_{3}^{4}
by
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}
in (
4.2
) because the commutator contains the terms like
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial\eta
,
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial v
and
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial Q
whose
L^{2}
-norm cannot be controlled in
H_{*}^{8}
. In specific, we have

\displaystyle\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\nabla_{A}^{i}f)=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}f)+(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}A^{li})\partial_{l}f+[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},A^{li},\partial_{l}f]

(5.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}f)-\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(A^{lr}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,A^{mi})\partial_{l}f+[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},A^{li},\partial_{l}f]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}f-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{r}\,A^{lr}\,\partial_{l}f)+\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{r}\,\nabla_{A}^{i}(\nabla_{A}^{r}f)-([\overline{\partial}{}^{7},A^{lr}A^{mi}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\partial_{l}f+[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},A^{li},\partial_{l}f].

We notice that the
L^{2}(\Omega)
-norm of the following quantities coming from the last two terms of (
5.2
) cannot be controlled because
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}
may fall on
A=\partial\eta\times\partial\eta
and
\partial f
.

\displaystyle e_{1}:=-\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(A^{lr}A^{mi})\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f,

\displaystyle e_{2}:=-7\overline{\partial}{}(A^{lr}A^{mi})\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f

(5.3)

\displaystyle e_{3}:=8(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{l}f),

\displaystyle e_{4}:=8(\overline{\partial}{}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}f).

Here
8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}
means there are 8 terms of the form
\overline{\partial}{}_{1}^{i_{1}}\overline{\partial}{}_{2}^{i_{2}}
with
i_{1}+i_{2}=7
. We will repeatedly use similar notations throughout the manuscript.

Our idea to overcome this difficulty is mainly based on the following three techniques:

1.

Modify the definition of “Alinhac good unknowns”: Rewrite these quantities in terms of
\nabla_{A}^{i}(\cdots)+~L^{2}
-bounded terms, and then merge the terms inside the covariant derivative
\nabla_{A}^{i}
into the “Alinhac good unknowns”.

2.

Produce a weighted normal derivative to replace a non-weighted one: There are terms like
(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{3}\eta)(\overline{\partial}{}Q)
. Since
Q|_{\Gamma}=0
, we know
\overline{\partial}{}Q|_{\Gamma}=0
. Therefore, we can estimate the
L^{\infty}
-norm of
\overline{\partial}{}Q
by fundamental theorem of calculus: (Suppose
y_{3}>0
without loss of generality)

|\overline{\partial}{}Q(t,y_{3})|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}=\left|0+\int_{1}^{y_{3}}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q(t,\zeta_{3})d\zeta_{3}\right|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}\leq(1-y_{3})\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\sigma(y_{3})\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q\|_{L^{\infty}},

then we move the
\sigma(y_{3})
to
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{3}\eta
to get a weighted normal derivative
(\sigma\partial_{3})^{1}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta
whose
L^{2}
-norm can be directly bounded in
H_{*}^{8}
.

3.

Replace
\nabla_{\hat{A}}Q
(contains a normal derivative) by
-\rho_{0}\partial_{t}v+(b_{0}\cdot\partial)(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)
(only contains tangential derivative) in order to make the order of the derivatives lower thanks to the anisotropy of
H_{*}^{m}
.

Now we analyze these extra terms from the commutator. We start with
8(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{l}f)
and
8(\overline{\partial}{}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}f)
coming from
[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},A^{li},\partial_{l}f]
in (
5.2
). Since
\overline{\partial}{}A^{li}=-A^{lp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi}
, we have

\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{li}=-A^{lp}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi}-[\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{lp}A^{mi}]\partial_{m}\eta_{p},

where the highest order term in
[\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{lp}A^{mi}]\partial_{m}\eta_{p}
is
\overline{\partial}{}^{6}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}
whose
L^{2}
-norm can be directly bounded by
\|\eta\|_{8,*}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle 8(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{l}f)=

\displaystyle-8(A^{mi}\,\partial_{m}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\,A^{lp})\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{l}f-8([\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{lp}A^{mi}]\partial_{m}\eta_{p})\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{l}f

(5.4)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-8\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\,A^{lp}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{l}f)\underbrace{+8\nabla_{A}^{i}(\nabla_{A}^{p}\overline{\partial}{}f)\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}-8([\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{lp}A^{mi}]\partial_{m}\eta_{p})\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{l}f}_{=:C_{1}(f)}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle-8\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}f)+C_{1}(f),

where
C_{1}(f)
can be controlled by using
H^{1/2}\hookrightarrow L^{3}
and
H^{1}\hookrightarrow L^{6}
in 3D domain

\displaystyle C_{1}(f)\lesssim

\displaystyle\|A\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial^{2}\overline{\partial}{}f\|_{L^{6}}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{L^{3}}+\|A\,\overline{\partial}{}f\,\partial A\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{L^{2}}+P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial f\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|A\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial^{2}\overline{\partial}{}f\|_{1}\|\langle\overline{\partial}{}\rangle^{1/2}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}+P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial f\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|A\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial^{2}\overline{\partial}{}f\|_{1}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}^{1/2}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}^{1/2}+P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial f\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\eta\|_{3})\|f\|_{7,*}\|\eta\|_{8,*}+P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial f\|_{L^{\infty}}.

The term
8(\overline{\partial}{}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}f)
should be treated differently in the case of
f=v_{i}
and
f=Q
respectively.

•

When
f=v_{i}
, then this term becomes

\displaystyle 8(\overline{\partial}{}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}v_{i})=

\displaystyle-8A^{lp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}v_{i}=-8A^{li}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{i}\,A^{mp}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}v_{p}

(5.5)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-8\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v_{p}\,A^{mp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{i})+\underbrace{8\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{i}\,A^{mp})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v_{p}}_{=:C_{2}(v)}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle-8\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i})+C_{2}(v),

and similarly we have
\|C_{2}(v)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|v\|_{8,*}
.

•

When
f=Q
, we cannot mimic the simplification as above. Instead, we need to invoke the MHD equation to replace
\nabla_{A}Q
by tangential derivatives. We consider

\displaystyle 8(J\overline{\partial}{}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}Q)=

\displaystyle-8(\hat{A}^{lp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}Q

(5.6)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(\hat{A}^{lp}\partial_{l}Q)\,A^{mi}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}+8(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\hat{A}^{lp})(\partial_{l}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})

\displaystyle+8\sum_{N=1}^{6}\binom{7}{N}(\overline{\partial}{}^{N}\hat{A}^{lp})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7-N}\partial_{l}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\left(\rho_{0}\partial_{t}v^{p}-(b_{0}\cdot\partial)(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta^{p})\right)A^{mi}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}+8(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\hat{A}^{lp})(\partial_{l}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})

\displaystyle+8\sum_{N=1}^{6}\binom{7}{N}(\overline{\partial}{}^{N}\hat{A}^{lp})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7-N}\partial_{l}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle C_{21}+C_{22}+C_{23}.

The
L^{2}
-norm of
C_{23}
can be directly controlled since the top order derivative is
\overline{\partial}{}^{6}\partial

\|C_{23}\|_{0}\lesssim\|\eta\|_{8,*}\|Q\|_{8,*}P(\|\eta\|_{7,*}).

(5.7)

The
L^{2}
-norm of
C_{22}
can be directly controlled when
l=3
because
\hat{A}^{3p}
consists of
\overline{\partial}{}\eta\times\overline{\partial}{}\eta
. When
l=1,2
, we need to invoke the second technique above, i.e., using
\overline{\partial}{}Q|_{\Gamma}=0
to produce a weight function
\sigma(y_{3})
.

\displaystyle\|C_{22}\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\hat{A}^{3p}\|_{0}\|\partial_{3}Q\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial\eta\,a\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\|(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\hat{A}^{Lp})(\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})\|_{0}

(5.8)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}\|Q\|_{3}+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\|(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\hat{A}^{Lp})(\sigma(y_{3})\partial_{3}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}\|Q\|_{3}+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\|\sigma\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\hat{A}^{Lp}\|_{0}\|(\partial_{3}\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{p}\,A^{mi})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|Q\|_{7,*}\left(\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}+\|(\sigma\partial_{3})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}\right),

where we use the fact that
\hat{A}^{Lp}
consists of
(\partial_{3}\eta)(\overline{\partial}{}\eta)
in the last step.

Finally,
C_{21}
can also be directly bounded because the top order derivatives are
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}
and
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)
. Note that
b_{0}^{3}|_{\Gamma}=0
yields the following estimates by using the second technique mentioned above.

\|b_{0}^{3}\partial_{3}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\|_{0}\lesssim\|\partial b_{0}\|_{2}\|(\sigma\partial_{3})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\|_{0},

and thus

C_{21}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})(\|\rho_{0}\|_{7,*}\|v\|_{8,*}+\|b_{0}\|_{7,*}\|(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{8,*}).

(5.9)

Therefore, we have the estimates for
C_{2}(Q):=8\overline{\partial}{}A^{li}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}Q

\|C_{2}(Q)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|b_{0}\|_{7,*},\|\rho_{0}\|_{7,*},\|\eta\|_{7,*})(\|\eta\|_{8,*}+\|v\|_{8,*}+\|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{8,*}+\|Q\|_{8,*}).

(5.10)

Next we analyze
-(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(A^{lr}A^{mi})~\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\partial_{l}f
coming from
-([\overline{\partial}{}^{7},A^{lr}A^{mi}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\partial_{l}f
. There are two terms of top order derivatives:

-\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(A^{lr}A^{mi})\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f=-(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{lr})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f-A^{lr}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{mi})\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f-\sum_{N=1}^{6}\binom{7}{N}(\overline{\partial}{}^{N}A^{lr})(\overline{\partial}{}^{6-N}A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f,

where the
L^{2}
-norm of the last term can be directly controlled

\left\|\sum_{N=1}^{6}\binom{7}{N}(\overline{\partial}{}^{N}A^{lr})(\overline{\partial}{}^{6-N}A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f\right\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|f\|_{3}.

Similarly as (
5.4
), the term
-A^{lr}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f
can be written as the covariant derivatives plus
L^{2}
-bounded terms

\displaystyle-A^{lr}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f=

\displaystyle A^{lr}A^{mp}(\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p})A^{ki}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f+([\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{mp}A^{ki}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{k}\eta_{p})A^{lr}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f

(5.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\,A^{mp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,A^{lr}\,\partial_{l}f)

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\,\nabla_{A}^{i}(A^{mp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,A^{lr}\,\partial_{l}f)+([\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{mp}A^{ki}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{k}\eta_{p})A^{lr}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f}_{=:C_{3}(f)}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}f)+C_{3}(f),

where
C_{3}(f)
can be directly controlled similarly as
C_{1}(f)

\|C_{3}(f)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\partial f\|_{2}.

We then compute
-(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{lr})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f
.

•

When
f=v_{i}
: Similarly as in (
5.11
), we have

\displaystyle-(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{lr})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}v_{i}=

\displaystyle A^{lp}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{k}\eta_{p})A^{kr}A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}v_{i}-([\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{lp}A^{kr}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{k}\eta_{p})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}v_{i}

(5.12)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A^{lp}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{k}\eta_{p})A^{ki}A^{mr}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{i}\,\partial_{l}v_{r}-([\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{lp}A^{kr}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{k}\eta_{p})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}v_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\,A^{lp}\,\partial_{l}v_{r}\,A^{mr}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{i})

\displaystyle\underbrace{-\nabla_{A}^{i}(A^{lp}A^{mr}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{i}\,\partial_{l}v_{r})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}-([\overline{\partial}{}^{6},A^{lp}A^{kr}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{k}\eta_{p})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}v_{i}}_{=:C_{4}(v)}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i})+C_{4}(v),

where
C_{4}(v)
can be directly controlled similarly as
C_{1}(f)

\|C_{4}(v)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\partial v\|_{2}.

•

When
f=Q
: If
l=3
, then this term can be directly controlled since
A^{3r}=J^{-1}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\times\overline{\partial}{}\eta
only contains first-order tangential derivatives. If
l=1,2
, then we can mimic the treatment of
C_{22}
, i.e., using
\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q|_{\Gamma}=0
and fundamental theorem of calculus to produce a weight function
\sigma(y_{3})
and move that to
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{lr}
. Define
C_{4}(Q):=-(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{lr})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}Q
, then

\displaystyle\|C_{4}(Q)\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{3r})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{3}Q\|_{0}+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\|(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{Lr})A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q\|_{0}

(5.13)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}\|Q\|_{3}P(\|\partial\eta\|_{2},\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial\eta\|_{2})+\sum_{L=1}^{2}\|\sigma\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{Lr}\|_{0}\|A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\overline{\partial}{}_{L}\partial_{3}Q\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}+\|(\sigma\partial_{3})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}\right)P(\|Q\|_{3},\|\overline{\partial}{}Q\|_{3},\|\eta\|_{7,*}).

Next we analyze
-7\overline{\partial}{}(A^{lr}A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}~\partial_{l}f
coming from
-[\overline{\partial}{}^{7},A^{lr}A^{mi}]\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}~\partial_{l}f
. This term cannot be directly controlled when
m=3
. We should analyze it term by term. First we have

\displaystyle-7\overline{\partial}{}(A^{lr}A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}~\partial_{l}f=

\displaystyle-7\overline{\partial}{}A^{lr}\,A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f-7A^{lr}\,\overline{\partial}{}A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 7A^{lp}\,\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{p}\,A^{kr}A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f+7A^{lr}A^{mp}\,\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{p}\,A^{ki}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}f.

The first term can be directly rewritten as follows

\displaystyle 7A^{lp}~\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{p}~A^{kr}A^{mi}~\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}~\partial_{l}f=

\displaystyle 7\nabla_{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{r}~A^{kr}~\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{p}~A^{lp}~\partial_{l}f)\underbrace{-7\nabla_{A}^{i}(A^{kr}~\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{p}~A^{lp}~\partial_{l}f)\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{r}}_{C_{5}(f)}

(5.14)

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle 7\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}f)+C_{5}(f),

where
C_{5}(f)
can be similarly controlled as
C_{1}(f)

\|C_{5}(f)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\partial f\|_{3}.

Then we analyze
7A^{lr}A^{mp}(\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{p})A^{ki}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\partial_{l}f
, which needs different treatment for
f=v_{i}
and
f=Q
respectively.

•

When
f=v_{i}
, we have the following simplification

\displaystyle 7A^{lr}A^{mp}\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{p}\,A^{ki}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}v_{i}=7A^{lr}A^{mi}\,\partial_{k}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i}\,A^{kp}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\partial_{l}v_{p}

(5.15)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 7\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{r}\,A^{lr}\,\partial_{l}v_{p}\,A^{kp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{k}\eta_{i})+\underbrace{7\nabla_{A}^{i}(A^{lr}\,\partial_{l}v_{p}\,A^{kp}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{k}\eta_{i})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{r}}_{C_{6}(v)}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle 7\nabla_{A}^{i}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i})+C_{6}(v),

and
\|C_{6}(v)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|\eta\|_{8,*}\|v\|_{3}
follows from direct computation, analogous to the analysis of the first term in
C_{1}(f)
.

•

When
f=Q
, this term becomes

\displaystyle C_{6}(Q):=

\displaystyle-7A^{lr}(\overline{\partial}{}A^{mi})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\partial_{l}Q

(5.16)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-7\left(\underbrace{\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(A^{lr}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})}_{=\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\delta^{l}_{m}=0}-(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{lr})(\partial_{m}\eta_{r})-\sum_{N=1}^{6}\binom{7}{N}(\overline{\partial}{}^{N}A^{lr})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7-N}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\right)\overline{\partial}{}A^{mi}\,\partial_{l}Q

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 7(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{3r})\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\overline{\partial}{}A^{mi}\,\partial_{3}Q+\sum_{L=1}^{2}(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{Lr})\partial_{m}\eta_{r}\,\overline{\partial}{}A^{mi}\,\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q

\displaystyle+7\sum_{N=1}^{6}\binom{7}{N}(\overline{\partial}{}^{N}A^{lr})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7-N}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\overline{\partial}{}A^{mi}\,\partial_{l}Q

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle C_{61}+C_{62}+C_{63}.

Since
A^{3r}=J^{-1}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\times\overline{\partial}{}\eta
, we know the top order term is of the form
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\times\overline{\partial}{}\eta
and thus
C_{61}
can be directly controlled

\|C_{61}\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{3})\|\eta\|_{8,*}\|\partial_{3}Q\|_{2}.

The term
C_{62}
can be treated in the same way as
C_{4}(Q)
in (
5.13
) by using
\overline{\partial}{}_{L}Q|_{\Gamma}=0
to produce a weight function
\sigma

\|C_{62}\|_{0}\lesssim(\|(\sigma\partial_{3})\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0})P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q\|_{2}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|Q\|_{7,*}\|\eta\|_{8,*}.

Finally,
C_{63}
can be directly controlled

\|C_{63}\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|\eta\|_{8,*}\|\partial Q\|_{2},

and thus

\|C_{6}(Q)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|\eta\|_{8,*}\|Q\|_{7,*}.

(5.17)

Now we plug (
5.4
)-(
5.5
), (
5.10
)-(
5.17
) into (
5.2
) and define the “modified Alinhac good unknowns” of
v
and
Q
with respect to
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}
as

\displaystyle\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}:=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v_{i}

\displaystyle-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}

(5.18)

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v_{i}-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i}

\displaystyle+\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}+\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i}

\displaystyle+7\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}+7\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i}

\displaystyle=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v_{i}

\displaystyle-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v_{i}-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i}+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i},

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{Q}^{*}:=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}Q

\displaystyle-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}Q-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}Q+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}Q.

(5.19)

Then the modified good unknowns satisfy the following relations

\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\text{div}_{A}v)=\nabla_{A}\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}+\sum_{M=0}^{6}C_{M}(v),~~~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\nabla_{A}Q)=\nabla_{A}\mathbf{Q}^{*}+\sum_{M=0}^{6}C_{M}(Q),

(5.20)

where
C_{0}(f)
comes from the directly controllable terms in the RHS of (
5.2
)

C_{0}(f):=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{r}~\nabla_{A}^{i}(\nabla_{A}^{r}f)-\sum_{N=2}^{6}\binom{7}{N}\overline{\partial}{}^{N}(A^{lr}A^{mi})\overline{\partial}{}^{7-N}(\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\partial_{l}f+\sum_{N=2}^{6}\binom{8}{N}(\overline{\partial}{}^{N}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{8-N}\partial_{l}f),

(5.21)

satisfies

\|C_{0}(f)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|f\|_{8,*},

and
C_{1}\sim C_{6}
are constructed in (
5.4
)-(
5.5
), (
5.10
)-(
5.17
).

5.2
Energy estimates of purely tangential derivatives

We denote
C^{*}(f):=C_{0}(f)+C_{1}(f)+\cdots+C_{6}(f)
and the “extra modification terms” in the modified Alinhac good unknowns by

\displaystyle(\Delta_{v}^{*})_{i}:=

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v_{i}-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i}+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{i},

\displaystyle\Delta_{Q}^{*}:=

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}Q+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}Q.

Then the modified Alinhac good unknowns become

\mathbf{V}^{*}=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v+\Delta_{v}^{*},~~\mathbf{Q}^{*}=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}Q-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}Q+\Delta_{Q}^{*}.

Remark
.

There are more modification terms in
\mathbf{V}^{*}
than in
\mathbf{Q}^{*}
. The reason is that we can replace
\nabla_{A}Q
which contains a normal derivative with tangential derivative (
\partial_{t}v
and
(b_{0}\cdot\partial)(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)
) by invoking the MHD equation. However, similar relation only holds for
\text{div}_{A}v
instead of
\nabla_{A}v
. Therefore, for those terms in the commutators containing
v
, we have to rewrite them to be the covariant derivatives of the modifition terms plus
L^{2}(\Omega)
-bounded terms.

It is straightforward to see that the
L^{2}(\Omega)
norms of
\Delta_{v}^{*},\Delta_{Q}^{*},\partial_{t}(\Delta_{v}^{*})
and
\partial_{t}(\Delta_{Q})
can be controlled by
P(\mathcal{E}(t))

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}(\Delta_{v}^{*})\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\|_{0}(\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v\|_{2}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\|_{2}\|\nabla_{A}v\|_{2})+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}v\|_{0}\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\|_{2}

(5.22)

\displaystyle+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}(\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{t}v\|_{2}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\|_{2}\|\nabla_{A}\partial_{t}v\|_{2}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v\|_{2}\|\nabla_{A}v\|_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\eta\|_{8,*},\|v\|_{8,*}),

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}(\Delta_{Q}^{*})\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\|_{0}(\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}Q\|_{2}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\|_{2}\|\nabla_{A}Q\|_{2})

(5.23)

\displaystyle+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}(\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{t}Q\|_{2}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\|_{2}\|\nabla_{A}\partial_{t}Q\|_{2}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v\|_{2}\|\nabla_{A}Q\|_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\eta\|_{8,*},\|v\|_{7,*},\|Q\|_{8,*}),

\|\Delta_{Q}^{*}\|_{0}+\|\Delta_{v}^{*}\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{7,*},\|v\|_{7,*},\|Q\|_{7,*}).

(5.24)

Now we take
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}
in the second equation of compressible MHD system (
1.17
) to get

R\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v)-J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)+\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\nabla_{A}Q)=\left[R,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\right]\partial_{t}v+\left[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\right]\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right).

Then invoking (
5.20
) to get

R\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v)-J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)+\nabla_{A}\mathbf{Q}^{*}=\left[R,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\right]\partial_{t}v+\left[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\right]\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)-C^{*}(Q).

Finally, plugging
\mathbf{V}^{*}=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v+\Delta_{v}^{*}
yields the evolution equation of
\mathbf{V}^{*}
and
\mathbf{Q}^{*}

\displaystyle R\partial_{t}\mathbf{V}^{*}-J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)+\nabla_{A}\mathbf{Q}^{*}=

\displaystyle\left[R,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\right]\partial_{t}v+\left[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\right]\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)

(5.25)

\displaystyle-C^{*}(Q)+R\partial_{t}(-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v+\Delta_{v}^{*})

We denote the RHS of (
5.25
) by
\mathbf{F}^{*}
. Similarly as in Section
4
, we compute the
L^{2}
-inner product of (
5.25
) and
J\mathbf{V}^{*}
to get the energy identity

\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Omega}\rho_{0}\left|\mathbf{V}^{*}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y=\int_{\Omega}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}-\int_{\Omega}(\nabla_{\hat{A}}\mathbf{Q}^{*})\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}+\int_{\Omega}J\mathbf{F}^{*}\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}.

(5.26)

5.2.1
Interior estimates

Using (
5.22
), the third integral on RHS of (
5.26
) is controlled by direct computation

\int_{\Omega}J\mathbf{F}^{*}\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}\lesssim\|J\mathbf{F}^{*}\|_{0}\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}\lesssim P\left(\left\|\left(\rho_{0},\eta,v,Q,b_{0},(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{8,*}\right)\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}.

(5.27)

The first integral on RHS of (
5.26
) can be similarly treated as (
4.9
)-(
4.13
) by replacing
\partial_{3}^{4}
by
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}
and
\|\cdot\|_{4}
-norm by
\|\cdot\|_{8,*}
-norm. We omit the details and list the result

\int_{\Omega}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}\,\mathrm{d}y\lesssim-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Omega}J\left|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y+K_{11}^{*}+P\left(\|(\eta,v,b_{0},(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\|_{8,*}\right),

(5.28)

where
K_{11}^{*}
is defined to be

K_{11}^{*}:=-\int_{\Omega}J\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)\cdot\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y.

(5.29)

Next we analyze the term
-\int_{\Omega}J\nabla_{A}\mathbf{Q}\cdot\mathbf{V}
. Integrating by parts and using Piola’s identity, we get

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}(\nabla_{\hat{A}}\mathbf{Q}^{*})\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}=\int_{\Omega}J\mathbf{Q}(\nabla_{A}\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*})-\int_{\Gamma}J\mathbf{Q}A^{li}N_{l}\mathbf{V}_{i}^{*}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}=:I^{*}+IB^{*}.

(5.30)

Invoking (
5.18
), (
5.20
) and
Q=q+\frac{1}{2}|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta|^{2}
, we get

\displaystyle I^{*}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}J\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y+\int_{\Omega}J\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\left(\frac{1}{2}\left|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right|^{2}\right)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y

(5.31)

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}(-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\,\partial_{l}Q+\Delta_{Q}^{*})\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y-\int_{\Omega}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}Q~C^{*}(v)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle I_{1}^{*}+I_{2}^{*}+I_{3}^{*}+I_{4}^{*},

where
I_{4}^{*}
can be directly controlled by using the estimates of
C^{*}(v)

I_{4}^{*}\lesssim\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}Q\|_{0}\|C^{*}(v)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*})\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}Q\|_{0}\|v\|_{8,*}.

(5.32)

Similarly,
I_{2}^{*}
produces another higher order term to cancel with
K_{11}^{*}

\displaystyle I_{2}=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\int_{\Omega}J\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\text{div}_{A}v)}_{\text{exactly cancel with }K_{11}^{*}}\,\mathrm{d}y

(5.33)

\displaystyle+\sum_{N=1}^{7}\binom{8}{N}\int_{\Omega}J\overline{\partial}{}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\overline{\partial}{}^{8-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}(\text{div}_{A}v)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-K_{11}^{*}-\sum_{N=1}^{7}\binom{8}{N}\int_{\Omega}\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\overline{\partial}{}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\overline{\partial}{}^{8-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\partial_{t}q\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle-\sum_{N=1}^{7}\binom{8}{N}\int_{\Omega}J\overline{\partial}{}^{N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\cdot\overline{\partial}{}^{8-N}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\left(\left[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right]\partial_{t}q\right)\,\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle-K_{11}^{*}+I_{21}^{*}+I_{22}^{*}

Similarly as in (
4.19
)-(
4.20
), the term
I_{21}^{*}
should be controlled by integrating
\partial_{t}
by parts under time integral and
I_{22}^{*}
can be directly controlled. We omit the details

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}I_{21}^{*}\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t

(5.34)

\displaystyle I_{22}^{*}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\|_{7,*}^{2}\|q\|_{8,*}.

(5.35)

The term
I_{1}^{*}
produces the energy term
\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}^{2}
as in (
4.17
).

I_{1}^{*}\lesssim-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Omega}\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q|^{2}+P(\|q\|_{8,*},\|\rho_{0}\|_{8,*},\|\eta\|_{8,*}).

(5.36)

I_{3}^{*}
can be controlled by integrating
\partial_{t}
by parts under time integral after invoking
\text{div}_{A}v=-\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\partial_{t}q
and (
5.23
)-(
5.24
).

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}I_{3}^{*}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\,\partial_{l}Q-\Delta_{Q}^{*})\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\partial_{t}q\,\mathrm{d}y+\underbrace{\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\,\partial_{l}Q-\Delta_{Q}^{*})\left(\left[\overline{\partial}{}^{8},\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\right]\partial_{t}q\right)}_{L_{2}^{*}}\,\mathrm{d}y

(5.37)

\displaystyle\overset{\partial_{t}}{=}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\left(\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\,\partial_{l}Q-\Delta_{Q}^{*}\right)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\,\mathrm{d}y+\int_{\Omega}\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,\hat{A}^{lp}\,\partial_{l}Q-\Delta_{Q}^{*})\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\,\mathrm{d}y\bigg|^{T}_{0}+L_{2}^{*}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}+\left(\left\|\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\,A\,\partial Q\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}+\|\Delta_{Q}^{*}\|_{0}\right)\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P\left(\|(\eta,v,q,\rho_{0})\|_{8,*}\right)\,\mathrm{d}t.

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}+\varepsilon\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\,A\,\partial Q\right\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}^{4}+\|\Delta_{Q}^{*}\|_{0}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}_{0}+\varepsilon\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\left\|\partial_{t}\left(\frac{JR^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\,A\,\partial Q\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v(t)\|_{0}^{4}+\|\partial_{t}(\Delta_{Q}^{*})\|_{0}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}t+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\varepsilon\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t,

Summarizing (
5.31
)-(
5.37
) and choosing
\varepsilon>0
to be sufficiently small, we get the estimates of
I^{*}
under time integral

\int_{0}^{T}I^{*}\,\mathrm{d}t\lesssim-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{J^{2}R^{\prime}(q)}{\rho_{0}}\left|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y\bigg|^{T}_{0}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(5.38)

5.2.2
Boundary estimates

Now it remains to deal with the boundary integral
IB^{*}
. Since
Q|_{\Gamma}=0
, we know

\mathbf{Q}^{*}|_{\Gamma}=-\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\,\partial_{3}Q+\Delta_{Q}^{*},

and

\Delta_{Q}^{*}|_{\Gamma}=-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}\,A^{3r}\,\partial_{3}Q.

Then the boundary integral
IB^{*}
reads

\displaystyle IB^{*}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\,\partial_{3}Q\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}-\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}A^{3p}\partial_{3}Q)(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i})\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

(5.39)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\Delta_{Q}^{*}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}+\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\Delta_{Q}^{*}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}\Delta_{Q}^{*}(\Delta_{v}^{*})_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}+\int_{\Gamma}\hat{A}^{3i}N_{3}(\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\,\partial_{3}Q)(\Delta_{v}^{*})_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IB_{1}^{*}+IB_{2}^{*}+IB_{3}^{*}+IB_{4}^{*}+IB_{5}^{*}+IB_{6}^{*}.

Before going to the proof, we would like to state our basic strategy to deal with the boundary control

•

IB_{1}^{*}
together with the Raylor-Taylor sign condition gives the boundary energy
|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}|_{0}^{2}
and the extra terms can be cancelled by
IB_{2}^{*}
. This step also appears in the study of Euler equations
[
8
,
13
,
41
,
42
,
45
]
and incompressible MHD
[
29
,
25
,
21
,
22
]
and compressible resistive MHD
[
72
]
. It actually gives the control of the second fundamental form of the free surface
[
8
]
.

•

IB_{3}^{*}
: We can write
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v_{i}=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}
and integrate
\partial_{t}
by parts. When
\partial_{t}
falls on
\Delta_{Q}^{*}
, the boundary integral can be directly controlled by using trace lemma. When
\partial_{t}
falls on
\hat{A}^{3i}
, such terms exactly cancel with the top order term in
IB_{4}^{*}
.

•

IB_{5}^{*}
and
IB_{6}^{*}
: Direct computation together with the trace lemma gives the control.

We first compute
IB_{1}^{*}
. Similarly as (
4.24
), we have

\displaystyle IB_{1}^{*}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\Gamma}\left(-\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)JA^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

(5.40)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int_{\Gamma}\left(-J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Gamma}\partial_{t}\left(J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}+\int_{\Gamma}\left(-J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)\partial_{t}A^{3i}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IB_{11}^{*}+IB_{12}^{*}+IB_{13}^{*},

The term
IB_{11}^{*}
together with the Rayleigh-Taylor sign condition gives the boundary energy

\int_{0}^{T}IB_{11}^{*}\leq-\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|^{T}_{0},

(5.41)

and
IB_{12}^{*}
can be directly controlled by the boundary energy

IB_{12}^{*}\lesssim\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\left|\partial_{t}\left(J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}\right)\right|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim P(\mathcal{E}(t)).

(5.42)

Then we plug
\partial_{t}A^{3i}=-A^{3r}\partial_{k}v_{r}A^{ki}
into
IB_{13}^{*}
to get the cancellation structure

\displaystyle IB_{13}^{*}=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}A^{3r}\,\partial_{k}v_{r}A^{ki}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}

(5.43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}J\frac{\partial Q}{\partial N}A^{3i}\,\partial_{k}v_{i}\,A^{kr}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}\,A^{3p}\,\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{r}=-IB_{2}^{*}

Next we analyze
IB_{3}^{*}
. We write
v_{i}=\partial_{t}\eta_{i}
and integrate this
\partial_{t}
by parts

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}IB_{3}^{*}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Gamma}JA^{3i}N_{3}\Delta_{Q}^{*}~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t

(5.44)

\displaystyle\overset{\partial_{t}}{=}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Gamma}J~\partial_{t}A^{3i}~N_{3}\Delta_{Q}^{*}~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Gamma}A^{3i}N_{3}~\partial_{t}(J\Delta_{Q}^{*})~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t-\int_{\Gamma}JA^{3i}N_{3}~\Delta_{Q}^{*}~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\bigg|^{T}_{0}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle IB_{31}^{*}+IB_{32}^{*}+IB_{33}^{*}.

Again, plug
\partial_{t}A^{3i}=-A^{3r}\partial_{k}v_{r}A^{ki}
into
IB_{31}^{*}
to get the cancellation with
IB_{4}^{*}

\displaystyle IB_{31}^{*}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Gamma}JA^{3r}~\partial_{k}v_{r}~A^{ki}~N_{3}~\Delta_{Q}^{*}~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t

(5.45)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Gamma}JA^{3i}~\partial_{k}v_{i}~A^{kr}~N_{3}~\Delta_{Q}^{*}~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{r}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t=-IB_{4}^{*}.

For
IB_{33}^{*}
, we use the fact that
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta|_{t=0}=0
(and thus
\Delta_{Q}^{*}|_{\Gamma}=0
when
t=0
) together with Lemma
3.1
to get

\displaystyle\int_{\Gamma}JA^{3i}N_{3}\Delta_{Q}^{*}~\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\bigg|_{t=T}=-\int_{\Gamma}JA^{3i}N_{3}(8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}~A^{3p}~\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~A^{3r}~\partial_{3}Q)\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

(5.46)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}|J|_{L^{\infty}}(|A^{3p}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q|_{L^{\infty}}+|(\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r})A^{3r}\partial_{3}Q|_{L^{\infty}})\int_{0}^{T}|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v(t)|_{0}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}P(\|\eta\|_{8,*},\|Q\|_{8,*})\int_{0}^{T}\|v(t)\|_{8,*}\,\mathrm{d}t.

In
IB_{32}^{*}
, we invoke the relation (
4.37
) to get

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(J\Delta_{Q}^{*})|_{\Gamma}=

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v_{p}\,\hat{A}^{3p}\,\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}\partial_{3}Q

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}~\partial_{t}(\hat{A}^{3p}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q)+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\partial_{t}(\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}\partial_{3}Q)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v_{p}~\hat{A}^{3p}~\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}\partial_{3}Q

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\partial_{t}\overline{\partial}{}(\hat{A}^{3p}\partial_{3}Q)+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}{}\hat{A}^{3p}~\partial_{3}Q)+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\partial_{t}(\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}~\partial_{3}Q)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{qbdry}}{=}

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v_{p}~\hat{A}^{3p}~\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}\partial_{3}Q

\displaystyle+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\partial_{t}\overline{\partial}{}\left(\rho_{0}\partial_{t}v^{p}-(b_{0}\cdot\overline{\partial}{})(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta)^{p}\right)+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}~\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}{}\hat{A}^{3p}~\partial_{3}Q)+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\partial_{t}(\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}\partial_{3}Q).

Then we use
H^{\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbb{T}^{2})\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2})
, Lemma
3.1
and standard Sobolev trace lemma to get

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}(J\Delta_{Q}^{*})|_{\Gamma}\right|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v|_{0}\left(|A~\overline{\partial}{}\partial Q|_{L^{\infty}}+|a~\overline{\partial}{}\partial\eta~A~\partial Q|_{L^{\infty}}\right)

(5.47)

\displaystyle+|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}|_{0}\left(\left|\rho_{0}\partial_{t}^{2}v^{p}+\partial_{t}(b_{0}\cdot\overline{\partial}{})b^{p}\right|_{W^{1,\infty}(\mathbb{T}^{2})}+\left|\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}{}\hat{A}^{3p}~\partial_{3}Q)+\partial_{t}(\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}~\partial_{3}Q)\right|_{L^{\infty}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|v\|_{8,*}\|\partial Q\|_{5,*}P(\|\partial\eta\|_{3})

\displaystyle+\|\eta\|_{8,*}\left(\|\rho_{0}\partial_{t}^{2}v+\partial_{t}(b_{0}\cdot\overline{\partial}{})b^{p}\|_{5,*}+\left\|\partial_{t}(\overline{\partial}{}\hat{A}^{3p}~\partial_{3}Q)+\partial_{t}(\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~\hat{A}^{3r}~\partial_{3}Q)\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\eta\|_{8,*}+\|v\|_{8,*}+\|b\|_{7,*})(\|Q,\eta,v\|_{7,*},\|b_{0},\rho_{0}\|_{3}),

and thus

IB_{32}^{*}\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}P(\|\eta\|_{8,*},\|v\|_{8,*},\|Q\|_{8,*},\|b\|_{8,*},\|\rho_{0}\|_{3})\,\mathrm{d}t.

(5.48)

From (
5.39
), we know it suffices to control the product of “error part”
IB_{5}^{*}

IB_{5}^{*}\lesssim|\hat{A}^{3i}|_{L^{\infty}}|\Delta_{Q}^{*}|_{\Gamma}|_{0}|(\Delta_{v}^{*})_{i}|_{0},

and the RHS can be directly controlled by Lemma
3.1
and standard trace lemma

\displaystyle\left|\Delta_{Q}^{*}|_{\Gamma}\right|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}\right|_{0}\left(|A^{3p}\overline{\partial}{}\partial_{3}Q|_{L^{\infty}}+|\nabla_{A}^{p}\overline{\partial}{}\eta_{r}~A^{3r}\partial_{3}Q|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\lesssim P(\|Q\|_{7,*},\|\eta\|_{7,*})\|\eta\|_{8,*},

\displaystyle\left|\Delta_{v}^{*}\right|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta|_{0}\left(|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v|_{L^{\infty}}+|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v|_{L^{\infty}}+|\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta|_{L^{\infty}}\right)+|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v|_{0}|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle P(\|\eta\|_{7,*})(\|v\|_{8,*}+\|v\|_{7,*}\|\eta\|_{8,*}).

Therefore,

IB_{5}^{*}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*},\|v\|_{8,*},\|Q\|_{7,*}),

(5.49)

and similarly

IB_{6}^{*}\lesssim|\hat{A}^{3i}\partial_{3}Q|_{L^{\infty}}|A^{3p}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{p}|_{0}|(\Delta_{v}^{*})_{i}|_{0}.

(5.50)

Summarizing (
5.39
)-(
5.50
) gives the control of the boundary integral

\int_{0}^{T}IB^{*}\lesssim-\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(5.51)

Combining (
5.26
), (
5.27
), (
5.28
), (
5.38
) and (
5.51
) and choosing
\varepsilon>0
in (
5.34
) to be suitably small, we get the following energy inequality

\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}^{2}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|_{t=T}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(5.52)

Finally, invoking (
5.18
), we get the
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}
-estimates of
v

\displaystyle\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta\|_{0}\|\nabla_{A}v_{i}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}\left(\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\|_{0}\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}}.

Since
\partial^{m}\eta|_{t=0}=0
for any
m\geq 2,m\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
, we know

\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v\|_{0}\lesssim\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}+P(\|v\|_{7,*},\|\eta\|_{7,*})\int_{0}^{T}P(\|v\|_{8,*}),

(5.53)

and thus

\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}v\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}^{2}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{8}q\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{8}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|_{t=T}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(5.54)

5.3
The case of one time derivative
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}

If we replace
\partial_{*}^{I}=\overline{\partial}{}^{8}
by
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}
, then most of steps in the proof above are still applicable because we do not integrate the derivative(s) in
\mathfrak{D}^{8}
by parts. However, we still need to do the following modifications due to the presence of time derivative.

5.3.1
Extra difficulty: non-vanishing initial data of
\partial_{*}^{I}\eta

If
\partial_{*}^{I}=\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}
, then we can no longer derive
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}\eta|_{t=0}=\mathbf{0}
from
\eta|_{t=0}=\text{Id}
due to the presence of time derivative and
\partial_{t}\eta=v
. This property is used in the analysis of
IB_{33}^{*}
and the control of the difference between
\mathbf{V}^{*}
and
\partial_{*}^{I}v
. Before we analyze the analogues of
IB_{33}^{*}
and (
5.53
) in the case of
\partial_{*}^{I}=\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}
, we have to find out the precise form of the modified Alinhac good unknowns when
\partial_{*}^{I}=\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}
.

5.3.2
The modified Alinhac good unknowns

Recall the “extra modification terms”
\Delta_{Q}^{*},\Delta_{v}^{*}
in (
5.25
) come from the bad terms (
5.3
). Now we replace
\overline{\partial}{}^{8}
by
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}
. In
e_{1},e_{2},e_{3}
in (
5.3
), if we replace
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}
by
\overline{\partial}{}^{6}\partial_{t}
(i.e., the time derivative falls on the higher order term), then their
L^{2}
norms can be directly controlled since
\partial_{t}a
has the same spatial regularity as
a
. Therefore, the remaining quantities whose
L^{2}
-norms cannot be directly controlled in the case of
\partial_{*}^{I}=\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}
are

\displaystyle e_{1}:=-\overline{\partial}{}^{7}(A^{lr}A^{mi})\partial_{t}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}~\partial_{l}f,

\displaystyle e_{2}:=-7\partial_{t}(A^{lr}A^{mi})~\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}~\partial_{l}f

(5.55)

\displaystyle e_{3}:=8(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}A^{li})\partial_{t}\partial_{l}f,

\displaystyle e_{4}:=(\partial_{t}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{l}f)+7(\overline{\partial}{}A^{li})(\overline{\partial}{}^{6}\partial_{t}\partial_{l}f).

Then the corresponding Alinhac good unknowns now becomes (with the abuse of terminology)

\mathbf{V}^{*}=\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}v-\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v+\Delta_{v}^{*},~~\mathbf{Q}^{*}=\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}Q-\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}Q+\Delta_{Q}^{*}.,

(5.56)

where

\displaystyle(\Delta_{v}^{*})_{i}:=

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\partial_{t}v_{i}-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i}+16\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}v_{i},

(5.57)

\displaystyle\Delta_{Q}^{*}:=

\displaystyle-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\partial_{t}Q+8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}v\cdot\nabla_{A}Q,

and

\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}(\text{div}_{A}v)=\nabla_{A}\cdot\mathbf{V}^{*}+C^{*}(v),~~~\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}(\nabla_{A}Q)=\nabla_{A}\mathbf{Q}^{*}+C^{*}(Q),

(5.58)

with

\|C^{*}(f)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\mathcal{E}(t))\|f\|_{8,*}.

Now, the analogue of
IB_{33}^{*}
becomes the following quantity (recall such term comes from the product of
\Delta_{Q}
and
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}v

\int_{\Gamma}JA^{3i}N_{3}(8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta_{p}~A^{3p}~\partial_{t}\partial_{3}Q-8\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\cdot\nabla_{A}\partial_{t}\eta_{r}~A^{3r}~\partial_{3}Q)\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime},

(5.59)

and we can still use
\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta|_{t=0}=\mathbf{0}
.

The analogue of (
5.53
) now needs some small modifications

\displaystyle\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}v\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\|_{0}\|\nabla_{A}v\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\eta\|_{0}\left(8\|\nabla_{A}\partial_{t}v\|_{L^{\infty}}+16\|\nabla_{A}v\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)

(5.60)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\|_{0}^{2}+\|\nabla_{A}v\|_{2}^{2}+\|\nabla_{A}\overline{\partial}{}v\|_{2}^{2}+\left(8\|\nabla_{A}\partial_{t}v\|_{L^{\infty}}+16\|\nabla_{A}v\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}v\|_{0}\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+\int_{0}^{T}P\left(\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}v\|_{0},\|\partial_{t}\partial v\|_{2},\|\partial_{t}\partial\overline{\partial}{}v\|_{2},\|\partial\overline{\partial}{}\eta\|_{2}^{2}\right)+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{V}^{*}\|_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

The remaining analysis should follow in the same way as in Section
5.2
, so we omit those details. The result is

\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}v\|_{0}^{2}+\left\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}\left(J^{-1}(b_{0}\cdot\partial)\eta\right)\right\|_{0}^{2}+\|\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}q\|_{0}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\left|A^{3i}\overline{\partial}{}^{7}\partial_{t}\eta_{i}\right|_{0}^{2}\bigg|_{t=T}\lesssim\mathcal{P}_{0}+P(\mathcal{E}(T))\int_{0}^{T}P(\mathcal{E}(t))\,\mathrm{d}t.

(5.61)

5.4
The case of 2
\sim
7 time derivatives

If the number of time derivatives in
\partial_{*}^{I}
is between 2 and 7, i.e,.
\partial_{*}^{I}
contains at least one spatial and two time derivatives, we can still mimic most steps in Section
5.3
. In this case we write
\partial_{*}^{I}=\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}
where
\mathfrak{D}=\overline{\partial}{}
or
\partial_{t}
and
\mathfrak{D}^{6}
contains at least one
\overline{\partial}{}
.

The extra time derivatives allow us to eliminate most of the “extra modification terms” in the modified Alinhac good unknowns as in (
5.25
), (
5.56
)-(
5.57
) and thus much simplify the analysis of Alinhac good unknowns and tne boundary control. The reason is that the
L^{2}
-norm of the analogues of
e_{1}\sim e_{3}
in (
5.3
) can be directly controlled in the case of
\mathfrak{D}^{8}=\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}
. In specific, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}(\nabla_{A}^{i}f)=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}f)+(\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}A^{li})\partial_{l}f+[\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2},A^{li},\partial_{l}f]

(5.62)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}f)-\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}(A^{lr}~\partial_{t}\partial_{m}\eta_{r}~A^{mi})\partial_{l}f+[\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2},A^{li},\partial_{l}f]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{A}^{i}(\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}f-\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}\eta_{r}~A^{lr}\partial_{l}f)+\underbrace{\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2}\eta_{r}~\nabla_{A}^{i}(\nabla_{A}^{r}f)-([\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t},A^{lr}A^{mi}]\partial_{t}\partial_{m}\eta_{r})\partial_{l}f}_{C_{0}(f)}+[\mathfrak{D}^{6}\partial_{t}^{2},A^{li},\partial_{l}f]

and

\|C_{0}(f)\|_{0}\lesssim P(\|\eta\|_{8,*},\|v\|_{8,*})\|f\|_{8,*}.

Therefore, the analogous analysis of
C_{1},C_{3}\sim C_{6}
in Section
5.1
are no longer needed here. The only problematic term is
